Let's design a strong optimizer:

Let's design a robust alternative algorithm from scratch:

1. Determine candidate lengths: if parent exists, use its length and also maybe 2x (if time). If no parent, use n = 32768 or 16384.

2. Generate initial sequences for each length:
   - Resampled parent (if any) and reversed? But convolution not symmetric, so reverse may be different? Reversing a changes polynomial: coefficients reversed? Score same because max of c of reversed? If a reversed, a*a coefficients reverse: c'_k = c_{2n-2-k}. Max same. So reverse same score. No use.
   - Multiplicative perturbations of parent.
   - Parametric shapes: f(x) times periodic oscillation. Maybe generate many and evaluate cheaply.
   - Random positive sequences with smoothing; but optimum likely not random.

3. Optimize each candidate with a combined algorithm:
   A. *Mirror descent with adaptive weights (softmax and top/excess)*:
      - Normalize sum=1.
      - Keep best.
      - Loop until deadline or no improvement.
      - Compute c=conv(a). D=max c.
      - Generate weights w:
         * softmax with beta: q_j = exp(beta*(c_j - D)) / Z. Choose beta by parameter.
         * top-k weights uniform or inverse gap.
         * excess above threshold T: max(0, c_j - T)^p normalized.
          We can cycle beta/threshold. For each weight, compute H=corr(a,w) (one FFT weighted? corr_pair via FFT each time). Then perform multiplicative update with a few step sizes:
             log_a_minus = log(a + eps) - eta * H / Hscale
             new_a = exp(log_minus - max), normalize.
         Evaluate score. Accept if improves.
      - If no improvement for some iterations, adapt beta/eta (increase beta for smoother? Actually larger beta focuses max; smaller distributes). Use schedule.
   B. *LSE-LBFGS/smooth projected gradient*: Use LSE weights and use `minimize`? Maybe custom FISTA better. But previous LSE-LBFGS likely works. We can implement a simple projected gradient with FISTA in log-simplex using LSE weights:
      1. Variables y_i = log a_i. Constraint g(y)=logsumexp(y)=0 for sum1. Projection to log-sum-exp=0: y <- y - logsumexp(y) (since exp normalized). This is not Euclidean but mirror.
      We can use mirror descent with momentum: maintain m, moment.
      However, we can use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with LSE objective and bounds to polish; it handles unbounded scale but may be slow. Custom line search multiplicative maybe enough.

   C. *Local coordinate ascent*: For each coordinate i, find optimum value x while others fixed maybe using 1D bisection to minimize max c. This can escape multiplicative local structures and drive some coefficients to zero. But O(n) per pass, each coordinate step requires updating all c? Could do if we can update c efficiently using FFT? Changing one a_i changes c_j by x * 2 a_{j-i} + x^2 delta_{2i}. We can compute for a given i the array of changes length 2n-1 O(n) to find max; doing for n coordinates O(n^2) impossible. But for random subset per iteration maybe.

   D. *Block optimization*: Choose block of B~32 consecutive coordinates. Fix others. We need optimize block to minimize max of c. Since B small, c changes: c = c0 + 2∑_{r in block} x_r a_{j-r} + ∑_{r,s in block} x_r x_s δ? Actually within block pairs add to c_{r+s} if both in block. This is small dense quadratic. Pseudo loop:
```
def optimize(a, deadline, rng, verbose):
    best_a=normalize(a); best_sc=2n*max(conv(best_a))
    a=best_a.copy(); c=conv(a); sc=best_sc
    eta=0.5; stag=0; best_time=0
    phase=0
    while time<deadline:
         # periodic adapt phase: cycle focus/flatten
         if phase%2==0: weights=focus_weights(c)
         else: weights=flatten_weights(c)
         H=corr(a,w)
         hscale=max(abs(H))
         if hscale < 1e-18: break or perturb
         accept=False
         # line search etas: try [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*2]
         for e in etas:
             new=mirror(a,H,e)
             c_new=conv(new)
             sc_new=2n*max(c_new)
             if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-14:
                 best...; a=new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new; eta=e; accept=True; stag=0; break
             elif sc_new < sc - 1e-12: # current improvement but not best?
                 a=new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new; stag=0; accept=True; # maybe not best? keep best separate. But if best_sc not updated, a not best; okay current may be better than previous but still above best.
                 break
         if not accept:
             stag +=1
             eta *=0.8
             if stag > maxstag:
                 # perturb
                 a = perturb(a,rng); c=conv(a); sc=2n*max(c)
                 if sc < best_sc: update
                 stag=0
                 eta=0.5
                 # maybe try random weights
         phase +=1
         if time since best >? maybe continue.
    return best
```
Need if current gets worse after perturb, best stays but current not best. Later improvements from worsened current may surpass best. We should maybe after many stagnant reset current to best before perturb? Could be beneficial to perturb best.

Perturb `perturb(a,rng,strength)`: as previous: multiplicative log noise with smoothing, or mix uniform. Ensure positive. Use several modes.

For far from target, small alpha flatten; near target, large alpha. The current score relative average: `score = 2n*cmax`. Lower bound ~1. If score >1.8 use alpha small/flatten; if <1.6 use larger focus. We can set alpha = 10 * (score - 1.0) maybe? For score 1.5 ->5, too small. Need α around 10-100. Let's maintain α cycle [2,5,10,30,100] and adapt.

Could evaluate a pool of weights with one corr each; maybe 3 weights per iteration to cover. Time still okay. def weight_from_d(d, style, alpha=None, frac=None, power=None, top_frac=None):
```
    if np.max(d)<=0: return uniform
    if style == 0: # softmax
       return softmax_weights_d(d, alpha)
    if style ==1: # excess
       return excess_weights_d(d, frac, power)
    if style==2: # top
       return top_weights_d(d, top_frac, weight_by='d')
    if style==3: # blend
       w1=softmax; w2=excess; mix=alpha? return mix*w1+(1-mix)*w2
```

Update line search:
```
def mirror_from_H(a,H,eta,eps=1e-300):
    hs=np.max(np.abs(H))
    if hs < 1e-300: return a
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,eps)) - (eta*H/hs)
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    new=np.exp(loga)
    s=new.sum()
    if s<=1e-300 or not finite: return a
    return new/s
```
Use H not abs in exponent; nonnegative. If H has negative (signed a), use H/hs but exponent may increase some; okay.

`line_search_update(a,H,eta_base,c_old_score,n)`:

Accept if new_sc < cur_sc - 1e-14 maybe. For best, update global separately. If new_sc < best_sc also set best. If none improves current but some < best_sc? Since current may be not best? We can always set current to best if not? Usually current=best. But after perturbation, current score might >best; if new <best but not <current? We can accept if new_sc < best_sc OR new_sc < cur_sc*(1-eps). If perturbed, current may be worse; improving towards best good. But if new_sc > best_sc but < cur_sc, accept to move.
- If no accept, return false.

The current score `cur_sc` should be from current c. For main self optimizer, after each accept, c = new_c. If no accept, keep c.

`optimize_self(a, deadline, rng, best_sc_holder, verbose)`: Use a dict/list for best? Top-level no closures; we can return best. But we need global best across lengths. We'll return best_sc,best_a.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_self(a0, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="self"):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a)
    c=self_conv(a); sc=2*n*np.max(c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    eta=0.5; stag=0; step=0; last_improve=time
    max_iter=None
    while time<deadline:
        # cycle weights; maybe if stag>Y use different
        style_idx = step if stag<20 else (step + stag//?) maybe random
        w = choose_style_weights(c, style_idx, rng, stag)
        H = corr_weights(a,w)
        hmax=np.max(np.abs(H))
        if hmax < 1e-20:
            # perturb
        accepted=False; accepted_eta=None
        # etas adapt: base list scaled by eta
        for e in (eta*0.5, eta, eta*2.0):
            if e < 1e-4: continue
            new=mirror(a,H,e)
            c_new=self_conv(new)
            sc_new=2*n*np.max(c_new)
            if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_a=new.copy(); best_sc=sc_new
                a=new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new; eta=e; accepted=True; break
            elif sc_new < sc - 1e-12:
                a=new; c=c_new; sc=sc_new; eta=e; accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
            stag=0
        else:
            stag +=1
            eta *=0.8
            if stag % 20 ==0:

                if time < deadline-30:
                    p=perturb(best_a, rng, 0.05+0.05*(stag/100))
                    cp=self_conv(p); scp=2*n*max(cp)
                    if scp < best_sc*1.02: # not too bad
                       a=p; c=cp; sc=scp; eta=0.3
                    # update best if better

                # reset current to best and retry with larger eta?
                a=best_a.copy(); c=self_conv(a); sc=best_sc; stag=0; eta=0.5
        step +=1
    return best_sc,best_a
```

Another possibly stronger and simpler improvement: Use *low-rank polynomial factorization*. Since coefficients nonnegative, perhaps we can find a sequence by solving for its square coefficient target via deconvolution. Suppose we want P(z)^2 ≈ (D/n)*(1+z+...+z^{2n-2}) plus edges. The DFT? Let c_target_j = D/(2n-1) constant? Nonnegative. We want P(z) with nonnegative coeffs. This is like finding a minimum-phase factor of a nearly constant polynomial. Could use spectral factorization: Let c_target(K) (same length) nonnegative. We can form its DFT; take square root phase to get P coefficients; then make nonnegative by taking abs? The coefficients of P after taking sqrt of autocorrelation are complex; if we take magnitudes maybe nonnegative? Let's explore.
For a real sequence a, its *autocorrelation* (with reversal) R_k=∑ a_i a_{i+k} has DFT |A(ω)|^2. But we have convolution c=P(z)^2, not autocorrelation. Its DFT at e^{iω} is P(e^{iω})^2. So if we choose P(e^{iω}) magnitude, the coefficients of P are inverse DFT of a phase. We can choose P(e^{iω}) = |Q(ω)| e^{iθ(ω)} where Q maybe sqrt of target coefficient DFT? Since c target flat has DFT C_target(ω)=∑ target_j e^{ijω}. We need P(ω)^2=C_target(ω). If C_target(ω) is positive real? For flat with edge? Not. But maybe we can choose a target c (nonnegative, constant) and compute square root in frequency domain: P_fft = sqrt(C_fft) (choosing phase 0) gives real? C_fft for a flat sequence length 2n-1 is e^{iω(N-1)} sinc, has negative values and phase. Square root may yield complex. The real part/abs can give candidate. Could be a feature.

Simpler: Use known "flat top window" filters: The sequence `a_i` could be a linear phase FIR whose squared magnitude response is flat. For nonnegative coefficients, maybe choose a_i = |h_i| where h is a window? Hmm.

Another analytic route: Use convolution of a sequence with itself. If a has widely separated positive spikes, cross terms at sums. This is like selecting weights for a sumset to balance. We could use greedy: start with a uniform, for each coefficient above target, reduce a pair products by alternating. Our optimizer essentially.

Let's focus on improving previous algorithm enough. The difference target 1.5027 vs 1.506439 is 0.25%; maybe a bit of hyperparameter tuning and larger n suffices. We can implement a comprehensive multi-start self/cross optimizer.

Need ensure code passes syntax, no closures, no evaluate import. It can call `evaluate_sequence` if defined. Use try/except.

Functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _np2(n):
    m = int(max(1, n))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

Clean/normalize:
```
def clean_nonnegative(x):
    arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    return arr

def normalize_sequence(x):
    arr=clean_nonnegative(x)
    if arr.size==0: return arr
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12:
       return np.ones(arr.size,dtype=np.float64)/float(arr.size)
    arr=arr/s
    # cap maybe 1000
    return np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
```
If arr contains all zeros after clip? fallback.

```
def self_fft_len(n): return _np2(2*n-1)
def corr_fft_len(n): return _np2(3*n-2) # for n + (2n-1)-1

def self_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    N=self_fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    out=out[:2*n-1]
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # For nonnegative, remove small negative due to roundoff: but keep large negative? self not. return np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```

`corr_with_weights(a,w)`:
```
def corr_with_weights(a,w):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return empty
    N=corr_fft_len(n)
    ar=a[::-1]
    A=np.fft.rfft(ar,N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
    full=full[:2*n-1]
    H=full[n-1:2*n-1]
    H=np.nan_to_num(H, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return H
```
Check length: `full[:2*n-1]` length 2n-1, slice n-1:2n-1 length n. Good. N may be 3n-2 next pow. For n=1? 3*1-2=1 next1. s ok.

```
    if cmax<0: cmax=0
    return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```

```
def excess_weights_from_d(d, frac, power):
    thr=1.0 - max(0.0,min(0.99,frac))

    if power !=1.0: ex=ex**power
    s=ex.sum()
```

```
def top_weights_from_d(d, top_frac, mode='d'):
```

`mirror_update(a,H,eta,eps=1e-300)`:
```
def mirror_update(a,H,eta,eps=1e-300):
    hs = np.max(np.abs(H))
    if hs < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hs): return a
    a_pos = np.maximum(a, eps)
    # clip exponent to avoid underflow/overflow
    delta = -eta * H / hs
    delta = np.clip(delta, -50.0, 50.0) # because relative to max H; if eta huge, 50 maybe too high? Use -80,80
    loga = np.log(a_pos) + delta
    loga -= np.max(loga)
    new = np.exp(loga)
    s = new.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return a
    return new / s
```
Since H nonnegative, delta <=0 (unless negative H). Clipping +50 could increase some coordinates; not harmful.

```
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-20
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

         best_a=par.copy(); best_sc=sc

  else:

  # create initial a for main: if parent resample to n_main else best profile

      a_main=best_a

  # allocate time: 70% self main, 20% cross, 10% high-n polish? Need robust.
  t_phase1 = t0 + 0.55*budget_s
  sc,a=optimize_self(a_main, t_phase1, rng, verbose=True, label="main")
  if sc<best_sc: update
  # cross alternating 20%
  t_cross = t0 + 0.75*budget_s
  if time < t_cross:
      # do several cross rounds from best
      for round in range(3):
          if time >= t_cross: break
          # create b perturb of best or if no, best
          b = perturb_sequence(best_a,rng,strength=0.05+0.05*round)
          rem=t_cross-time
          scb,bb=optimize_cross(best_a,b,min(t_cross, time+rem*0.6), rng)
          if scb<best_sc: update
          # also maybe optimize b against best and update
  # high-n resample if time and n_main < 65536
  n_high = n_main*2
  if n_high <= 65536 and time < deadline - 250 and best_a is not None:
       high = resample_sequence(best_a,n_high)
       sc_high=normalized_score(high)
       if sc_high < best_sc*1.005: # promising
          sc_high, high_a = optimize_self(high, deadline-100, rng)
          if sc_high<best_sc: update
  # final self polish
  if time < deadline-60 and best_a is not None:
       sc,a=optimize_self(best_a, deadline-20, rng)
       if sc<best_sc: update
  # final validate using evaluate_sequence
  out = finalize(best_a)
  try verified=evaluate_sequence(out)
  except: verified=best_sc
  print...
  return out
```

Now, let's verify derivative/sign for mirror update. H=corr(a,w) = ∑ a_i w_{r+i}. This is half gradient of weighted c wrt a_r. We update a_r *= exp(-eta H_r/hmax). Since gradient positive, this reduces coordinates that appear in high coefficients. Good.

For cross update, fixed f, H=corr(f,w) = ∑ f_i w_{r+i}. c_cross_r? c_j=∑ f_i x_{j-i}; ∂/∂x_r =∑ f_{j-r} w_j = w conv f? Is that `corr_with_weights(f,w)`? Our corr_with_weights(a,w) returns ∑ a_i w_{r+i}. For a=f, H_r=∑ f_i w_{r+i}. But ∂c_j/∂x_r =∑_{j} w_j f_{j-r} (since j-i=r -> i=j-r). This is ∑ f_s w_{s+r}. Same! H_r = ∑ f_s w_{s+r}. Yes good. So cross update uses same H.

For self, c_j=∑ a_i a_{j-i}; ∂c_j/∂a_r =∑ a_{j-r} w_j + ∑ a_{j-r} w_j =2∑ a_s w_{s+r}. Same H. Good.

Detailed function implementations:

```
def resample_sequence(arr, n):
    arr = clean_nonnegative(arr)
    m = arr.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)/float(max(1,n))
    if m == int(n):
        s=arr.sum()
        if s<1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
        return arr/s
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,int(n))
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    y = np.maximum(y,0.0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
    return y/s
```

Need `normalize_weight`:
```
def normalize_weight(w):
   w=np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   w=np.maximum(w,0.0)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite:
       n=w.size; return np.full(n,1/n)
   return w/s
```

`try_self_step` signature:
```
def try_self_step(a,c,d,cur_sc,best_sc,style,score,eta):
    w=make_weight(d,style,score)
    H=corr_with_weights(a,w)
    hmax = np.max(np.abs(H))
    if hmax <= 1e-300: return None
    etas=[]
    base=float(eta)
    for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0):
       e=base*mult
       if e>=1e-5 and e<=20.0: etas.append(e)
    # remove duplicates
    best_local=None
    for e in etas:
       new=mirror_update(a,H,e)
       if new is a? maybe if same skip
       cnew=self_convolution(new)
       scnew=normalized_score(new,cnew)
       if scnew < best_sc - 1e-13 or scnew < cur_sc - 1e-12:

               # If improves global, can break? still maybe another eta better. Limit 4 evals; continue maybe.
               if scnew < best_sc - 1e-13:
                   # Found global improvement; maybe continue? For speed break? Could miss slightly better but ok. break to accept.
                   break
    if best_local is None: return None
    return best_local
```

```
def optimize_self(a0, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="opt"):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    c=self_convolution(a)
    sc=normalized_score(a,c)
    if not finite: fallback uniform? return inf, uniform
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    eta=0.5
    stag=0
    step=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    n=a.size
    order=STYLE_ORDER
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        d = c / np.max(c) if max>0 else ones
        # choose styles
        style1=order[step % len(order)]
        style2=order[(step*5 + 3) % len(order)] maybe
        accepted=False
        for style in (style1, style2 if step%2==0 else style1):
             cand=try_self_step(a,c,d,sc,best_sc,style,sc,eta)
             if cand is not None:
                 a,c,sc,e = cand
                 if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                     best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
                 eta=e
                 accepted=True; stag=0
                 if verbose and time-last_print>30: print; last_print=time
                 break
        if not accepted:
             stag +=1
             eta *=0.8
             if eta < 1e-4: eta=1e-4
             if stag > 40:
                 # perturb best or current? If sc > best_sc*1.01, reset to best first
                 if sc > best_sc*1.01:
                     a=best_a.copy(); c=self_convolution(a); sc=best_sc; eta=0.3
                 else:
                     p=perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.03+0.15*min(1.0, stag/100.0))
                     cp=self_convolution(p); scp=normalized_score(p,cp)
                     if scp < best_sc:
                         best_a=p.copy(); best_sc=scp
                     if scp < best_sc*1.05 and time < deadline-60:
                         a=p; c=cp; sc=scp
                     else:
                         a=best_a.copy(); c=self_convolution(a); sc=best_sc
                     eta=0.4
                 stag=0
             # periodically reset eta upward to escape small step scale
             if stag % 100 == 0:
                 eta=0.5
        step +=1
        # Hard cap if loop exceeds deadline? while checks.
    if sc < best_sc: best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    return best_sc,best_a
```
If `try_self_step` returns a candidate improving current but not best, we set current to it. It may be below best? update. Good.

Perturb function can produce a sequence with zeros; normalize. We need no nested functions. Code:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    n=a.size
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if mode ==0:
       y=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
       noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
       k=int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n//100)))
       if k<=4: k=5
       if k%2==0: k+=1
       if k>n: k=n if n%2==1 else n-1
       if k<5: k=5 if n>=5 else 1
       kernel=np.ones(k)/k
       noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,mode='same')
       std=np.std(noise)
       if std>0: noise=noise/std
       noise-=noise.mean()
       y += float(strength)*noise
       out=np.exp(y-np.max(y))
    elif mode==1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))
       out=(1-t)*a + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode==2:
       out=a*np.exp(float(strength)*0.5*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode==3:
       idx=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       r=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//64), max(2,n//16)))
       out=a.copy()
       # block boost/damp
       out[idx:(idx+r)%n]? If wrap, simpler use modulo mask.
       for? avoid loop large. Use np.concatenate? Maybe skip.
       out = np.roll(a, idx) * block pattern.
    else:
       out=a*np.exp(float(strength)*0.3*(rng.random(n)-0.5)*2)
    return normalize_sequence(out)
```
For mode3, use rolling and repeat block:
```
off=int(rng.integers(0,n)); bl=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//64), max(2,n//16)))
pattern=1.0 + float(strength)*0.5*(rng.random(bl)*2-1)
full=np.repeat(pattern, n//bl + 1)
full=full[:n]
out=np.roll(a,off)*full
```
But `np.roll` cyclic; okay. Ensure no loop.

`normalize_sequence` clips zeros.

```python
"""
Search improved for minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.  The implementation combines:
  - multiplicative entropy mirror descent on the simplex,
  - adaptive active-set weights (softmax/excess/top-few/blends),
  - alternating cross-convolution convex response optimization,
  - multi-length resampling trials (larger sequences are often better),
  - gentle final LSE polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


STYLE_ORDER = [16, 2, 12, 3, 6, 1, 4, 7, 10, 0, 8, 5, 13, 11, 14, 15, 9]


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clean_nonnegative(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def normalize_sequence(x):
    arr = clean_nonnegative(x)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n == 0:
        return arr
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    arr = arr / s
    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)


def resample_sequence(arr, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    arr = clean_nonnegative(arr)
    m = int(arr.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_sequence(arr)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    y = np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return y / s


def self_fft_len(n):
    return next_power_of_two(2 * int(n) - 1)


def corr_fft_len(n):
    return next_power_of_two(3 * int(n) - 2)


def self_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = self_fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    out = out[:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def cross_convolution_transformed(fa, x, N):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * X, N)
    L = 2 * int(x.size) - 1
    out = out[:L]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def corr_with_weights(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = corr_fft_len(n)
    ar = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    full = full[:2 * n - 1]
    H = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    H = np.nan_to_num(H, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return H


def normalized_score(a, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if c is None:
        c = self_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def normalize_weight(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        n = int(w.size)
        if n == 0:
            return w
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def softmax_weights_from_d(d, alpha):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    mx = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(d.size, 1.0 / max(1, d.size))
    z = alpha * (d - mx)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(d.size)
        w[np.isclose(d, mx, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-15)] = 1.0
        return normalize_weight(w)
    return e / s


def excess_weights_from_d(d, frac, power):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    frac = float(np.clip(frac, 0.0005, 0.95))
    thr = 1.0 - frac
    ex = np.maximum(d - thr, 0.0)
    if float(power) != 1.0:
        ex = np.power(ex, float(power))
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 20.0)
    return ex / s


def top_weights_from_d(d, frac, by_weight):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(d.size)
    k = int(max(1, min(m, round(float(frac) * m))))
    if k <= 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / max(1, m))
    if k >= m:
        w = d.copy() if by_weight else np.ones(m)
        return normalize_weight(w)
    idx = np.argpartition(d, m - k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if by_weight:
        w[idx] = d[idx]
    else:
        w[idx] = 1.0
    return normalize_weight(w)


def blend_weights_from_d(d, alpha, frac, power, mix):
    w1 = softmax_weights_from_d(d, alpha)
    w2 = excess_weights_from_d(d, frac, power)
    mix = float(np.clip(mix, 0.0, 1.0))
    return normalize_weight(mix * w1 + (1.0 - mix) * w2)


def make_weight(d, style, score):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    mx = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if d.size == 0 or mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(max(1, d.size), 1.0 / max(1, d.size))
    d = d / mx

    if style == 0:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 1.0)
    if style == 1:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 3.0)
    if style == 2:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 10.0)
    if style == 3:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 30.0)
    if style == 4:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 100.0)
    if style == 5:
        return softmax_weights_from_d(d, 300.0)
    if style == 6:
        return excess_weights_from_d(d, 0.03, 1.0)
    if style == 7:
        return excess_weights_from_d(d, 0.08, 1.0)
    if style == 8:
        return excess_weights_from_d(d, 0.15, 2.0)
    if style == 9:
        return top_weights_from_d(d, 0.001, True)
    if style == 10:
        return top_weights_from_d(d, 0.005, False)
    if style == 11:
        return top_weights_from_d(d, 0.02, False)
    if style == 12:
        return blend_weights_from_d(d, 5.0, 0.05, 1.0, 0.5)
    if style == 13:
        return blend_weights_from_d(d, 50.0, 0.10, 1.0, 0.7)
    if style == 14:
        return top_weights_from_d(d, 0.05, True)
    if style == 15:
        return blend_weights_from_d(d, 2.0, 0.20, 1.0, 0.4)

    # Style 16: adaptive blend; sharper as the score approaches the target.
    if score > 1.80:
        alpha = 1.0
        frac = 0.25
        mix = 0.5
    elif score > 1.60:
        alpha = 3.0
        frac = 0.15
        mix = 0.5
    elif score > 1.55:
        alpha = 10.0
        frac = 0.08
        mix = 0.5
    elif score > 1.51:
        alpha = 50.0
        frac = 0.04
        mix = 0.6
    else:
        alpha = 200.0
        frac = 0.02
        mix = 0.7
    return blend_weights_from_d(d, alpha, frac, 1.0, mix)


def mirror_update(a, H, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    H = np.asarray(H, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or H.size == 0 or a.size != H.size:
        return a
    hs = float(np.max(np.abs(H)))
    if hs <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hs):
        return a
    delta = -float(eta) * H / hs
    delta = np.clip(delta, -80.0, 80.0)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) + delta
    loga -= float(np.max(loga))
    out = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a
    return out / s


def try_self_step(a, c, d, cur_sc, best_sc, style, score, eta):
    w = make_weight(d, style, score)
    H = corr_with_weights(a, w)
    etas = []
    base = float(eta)
    for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0):
        e = base * mult
        if e >= 1e-5 and e <= 20.0:
            etas.append(e)
    if not etas:
        return None

    best_local = None
    for e in etas:
        na = mirror_update(a, H, e)
        nc = self_convolution(na)
        ns = normalized_score(na, nc)
        if not np.isfinite(ns):
            continue
        if ns < cur_sc - 1e-12 or ns < best_sc - 1e-13:
            if best_local is None or ns < best_local[2]:
                best_local = (na, nc, ns, e)
            if ns < best_sc - 1e-13:
                break
    return best_local


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    strength = float(np.clip(strength, 0.01, 2.0))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

    if mode == 0:
        y = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n // 100)))
        if k < 5:
            k = 5
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k > n:
            k = n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1
        if k < 5:
            k = 5 if n >= 5 else 1
        kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
        sd = float(np.std(noise))
        if sd > 1e-15:
            noise = noise / sd
        else:
            noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
        y += strength * 0.75 * noise
        out = np.exp(y - float(np.max(y)))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        out = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        out = a * np.exp(0.6 * strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        off = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        bl = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 64), max(2, n // 16)))
        pattern = 1.0 + 0.5 * strength * (rng.random(bl) * 2.0 - 1.0)
        repeated = np.repeat(pattern, n // bl + 1)[:n]
        out = np.roll(a, off) * repeated
    else:
        out = a * np.exp(0.45 * strength * (rng.random(n) * 2.0 - 1.0))

    return normalize_sequence(out)


def generate_initial(n, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_sc = normalized_score(best_seq)
    considered = 0

    def_try = 1
    while def_try:
        break

    envs = []
    envs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    envs.append(x + 1e-3)
    envs.append((1.0 - x) + 1e-3)
    envs.append(np.sqrt(x + 1e-6))
    envs.append(np.sqrt(1.0 - x + 1e-6))
    envs.append(np.exp(-2.0 * x) + 0.1)
    envs.append(np.exp(-2.0 * (1.0 - x)) + 0.1)

    for env in envs:
        if considered > 80:
            break
        for cycles in (1, 2, 3, 4, 8, 16, 32):
            if considered > 90:
                break
            for amp in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                p = normalize_sequence(env * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * cycles * x + ph)))
                s = normalized_score(p)
                if s < best_sc:
                    best_sc, best_seq = s, p
                considered += 1

                p = normalize_sequence(env * (1.0 + amp * np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * cycles * x))))
                s = normalized_score(p)
                if s < best_sc:
                    best_sc, best_seq = s, p
                considered += 1

    for pw1 in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        for pw2 in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            if considered > 110:
                break
            arr = np.power(x + 1e-6, pw1) * np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-6, pw2)
            p = normalize_sequence(arr)
            s = normalized_score(p)
            if s < best_sc:
                best_sc, best_seq = s, p
            considered += 1

    for _ in range(6):
        if considered > 130:
            break
        Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * float(Nf)
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.5))
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** alpha)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
        p = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n) + amp * noise)
        s = normalized_score(p)
        if s < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_seq = s, p
        considered += 1

    for _ in range(4):
        if considered > 140:
            break
        p = resample_sequence(rng.random(n) + 0.05, n)
        s = normalized_score(p)
        if s < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_seq = s, p
        considered += 1

    return best_seq


def optimize_self(a0, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="self"):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    c = self_convolution(a)
    sc = normalized_score(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
        c = self_convolution(a)
        sc = normalized_score(a, c)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    eta = 0.5
    stag = 0
    step = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    order = STYLE_ORDER

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if cmax > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cmax):
            d = c / cmax
        else:
            d = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)

        style1 = order[step % len(order)]
        style2 = order[(step * 5 + 3) % len(order)]

        accepted = False
        style_candidates = (style1,) if (step % 3 != 0) else (style1, style2)
        for st in style_candidates:
            cand = try_self_step(a, c, d, sc, best_sc, st, sc, eta)
            if cand is not None:
                na, nc, ns, neta = cand
                if ns < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_a = na.copy()
                    best_sc = ns
                a = na
                c = nc
                sc = ns
                eta = neta
                accepted = True
                stag = 0
                if verbose and (time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0):
                    print("  [{}] accepted score={:.10f} eta={:.5g}".format(label, best_sc, eta))
                    last_print = time.monotonic()
                break

        if not accepted:
            stag += 1
            eta *= 0.8
            if eta < 1e-4:
                eta = 1e-4

            if stag > 40:
                if sc > best_sc * 1.01:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    c = self_convolution(a)
                    sc = best_sc
                    eta = 0.3
                else:
                    strength = 0.03 + 0.15 * min(1.0, float(stag) / 120.0)
                    p = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, strength)
                    cp = self_convolution(p)
                    scp = normalized_score(p, cp)
                    if scp < best_sc:
                        best_a = p.copy()
                        best_sc = scp
                    if scp < best_sc * 1.05 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
                        a = p
                        c = cp
                        sc = scp
                    else:
                        a = best_a.copy()
                        c = self_convolution(a)
                        sc = best_sc
                    eta = 0.4
                stag = 0

            if stag % 120 == 0:
                eta = 0.5

        step += 1
        if stag > 360:
            a = best_a.copy()
            c = self_convolution(a)
            sc = best_sc
            stag = 0
            eta = 0.5

    if sc < best_sc:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = sc
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def try_cross_step(x, f, fa, N, c, cross, d, eta):
    mx = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        d = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
    style = 16
    score = 1.5
    w = make_weight(d, style, score)
    H = corr_with_weights(f, w)
    etas = []
    base = float(eta)
    for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0):
        e = base * mult
        if e >= 1e-5 and e <= 20.0:
            etas.append(e)
    if not etas:
        return None

    best_local = None
    for e in etas:
        nx = mirror_update(x, H, e)
        nc = cross_convolution_transformed(fa, nx, N)
        cm = float(np.max(nc)) if nc.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            continue
        if cm < cross - 1e-16:
            if best_local is None or cm < best_local[2]:
                best_local = (nx, nc, cm, e)
            if cm < cross * 0.999999:
                break
    return best_local


def optimize_cross(fixed, var, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="cross"):
    f = normalize_sequence(fixed)
    x = normalize_sequence(var)
    n = int(x.size)
    N = self_fft_len(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(f.astype(np.float64), N)

    c = cross_convolution_transformed(fa, x, N)
    cross = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

    self_c = self_convolution(x)
    best_score = normalized_score(x, self_c)
    best_x = x.copy()

    eta = 0.5
    stag = 0
    step = 0
    accepted_count = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if cmax > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cmax):
            d = c / cmax
        else:
            d = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)

        accepted = False
        style_candidates = (16, 2) if step % 2 == 0 else (13, 4)
        for st in style_candidates:
            tmp = try_cross_step(x, f, fa, N, c, cross, d, eta)
            if tmp is not None:
                x, c, cross, neta = tmp
                eta = neta
                accepted = True
                accepted_count += 1
                stag = 0

                if accepted_count % 8 == 0 or cross < 0.5 / max(1, n):
                    self_c = self_convolution(x)
                    self_s = normalized_score(x, self_c)
                    if self_s < best_score:
                        best_score = self_s
                        best_x = x.copy()
                        if verbose and (time.monotonic() - last_print > 60.0):
                            print("  [{}] cross self-score={:.10f}".format(label, best_score))
                            last_print = time.monotonic()
                break

        if not accepted:
            stag += 1
            eta *= 0.75
            if eta < 1e-5:
                eta = 1e-5

            if stag > 35:
                p = perturb_sequence(best_x, rng, 0.05)
                cp = cross_convolution_transformed(fa, p, N)
                cm = float(np.max(cp)) if cp.size else 0.0
                spc = self_convolution(p)
                sp = normalized_score(p, spc)
                if sp < best_score:
                    best_score = sp
                    best_x = p.copy()
                if (not np.isfinite(cm)) or cm >= cross:
                    x = best_x.copy()
                    c = cross_convolution_transformed(fa, x, N)
                    cross = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                else:
                    x = p
                    c = cp
                    cross = cm
                eta = 0.4
                stag = 0

        step += 1

    self_c = self_convolution(best_x)
    best_score = normalized_score(best_x, self_c)
    return float(best_score), best_x


def polish_self(a0, deadline, rng, verbose=False, label="polish"):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    c = self_convolution(a)
    sc = normalized_score(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf"), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = sc
    eta = 0.04
    stag = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if cmax > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cmax):
            d = c / cmax
        else:
            d = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)

        cand = try_self_step(a, c, d, sc, best_sc, 16, sc, eta)
        if cand is not None:
            na, nc, ns, neta = cand
            if ns < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_a = na.copy()
                best_sc = ns
            a = na
            c = nc
            sc = ns
            eta = min(0.25, max(1e-5, neta * 1.1))
            stag = 0
            if verbose and (time.monotonic() - last_print > 60.0):
                print("  [{}] polished score={:.10f} eta={:.5g}".format(label, best_sc, eta))
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            stag += 1
            eta *= 0.7
            if eta < 1e-6:
                eta = 1e-6
            if stag > 120:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = self_convolution(a)
                sc = best_sc
                eta = 0.03
                stag = 0

    if sc < best_sc:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = sc
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def candidate_lengths_from_parent(parent_n):
    s = {32768}
    if parent_n is not None:
        pn = int(parent_n)
        if 256 <= pn <= 65536:
            s.add(pn)
        if pn < 16384:
            s.add(16384)
        if pn < 32768:
            s.add(32768)
        if pn < 65536:
            s.add(65536)
    out = []
    for v in s:
        v = int(v)
        if 1024 <= v <= 65536:
            out.append(v)
    out.sort()
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - 10.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    def consider(seq):
        nonlocal_best = consider  # placeholder to satisfy no-nested? This comment only.

    # The above nested name is not used; actual logic below uses local updates.
    cand_scores = []

    if parent is not None:
        lengths = candidate_lengths_from_parent(parent.size)
        # If parent is huge, evaluate only it and a capped 32768/65536.
        for L in lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > t0 + 120:
                break
            trial = resample_sequence(parent, L)
            tr_sc = normalized_score(trial)
            cand_scores.append((tr_sc, trial))
        if cand_scores:
            cand_scores.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            for sc, seq in cand_scores:
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = seq.copy()
    else:
        lengths = [32768]

    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        if parent is None and total >= 600:
            # Quick bootstrap at moderate length if no parent is available.
            n_boot = 4096
            boot_dead = min(hard_deadline, t0 + max(45.0, 0.04 * total))
            init = generate_initial(n_boot, rng)
            sc_boot, boot_seq = optimize_self(init, boot_dead, rng, verbose=False, label="boot")
            if np.isfinite(sc_boot) and (not np.isfinite(best_sc) or sc_boot < best_sc):
                best_sc = sc_boot
                best_a = boot_seq.copy()
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60:
                out = finalize_for_return(best_a)
                return out
            main_len = 32768
            main_a = resample_sequence(boot_seq, main_len)
        else:
            main_len = 32768
            main_a = generate_initial(main_len, rng)
            sc0 = normalized_score(main_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc0
                best_a = main_a.copy()

    if best_a is not None:
        # Keep the best trial if it is at a length different from parent-resampled main length.
        pass

    # Ensure a main sequence exists for the heavy phase.
    if best_a is None:
        main_a = np.ones(32768, dtype=np.float64) / 32768.0
        best_a = main_a.copy()
        best_sc = normalized_score(best_a)

    # Choose the main optimization sequence: prefer the best trial, but cap very large lengths
    # unless they are already clearly better.
    if parent is not None and cand_scores:
        # cand_scores sorted by score.
        for sc, seq in cand_scores:
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_sc * 1.001 and seq.size <= 65536:
                main_a = seq.copy()
                break
        else:
            main_a = best_a.copy()
    else:
        main_a = best_a.copy()

    # If the parent itself is available and its original score is very good, start main from
    # the best candidate trial only if its length is not larger than 65536.
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        main_a = generate_initial(32768, rng)
        best_sc = normalized_score(main_a)
        best_a = main_a.copy()

    # Phase 1: main multiplicative optimization (~50%).
    phase1_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 240, t0 + 0.50 * total)
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_deadline:
        sc_main, seq_main = optimize_self(main_a, phase1_deadline, rng, verbose=True, label="main")
        if sc_main < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_main
            best_a = seq_main.copy()
            print("Main done: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    # Phase 2: alternating cross-convolution response optimization (~15%).
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 240:
        cross_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 240, t0 + 0.65 * total)
        chunks = 3
        for r in range(chunks):
            if time.monotonic() >= cross_deadline or time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 240:
                break
            rem = cross_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            end = min(cross_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, rem / float(chunks - r) * 0.85))
            b0 = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.04 + 0.04 * r)
            sc_cross, seq_cross = optimize_cross(best_a, b0, end, rng, verbose=False, label="cross")
            if sc_cross < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_cross
                best_a = seq_cross.copy()
                print("Cross done: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    # Phase 3: try a larger length by resampling, if time and candidate look promising (~20%).
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120:
        base_n = int(best_a.size)
        if base_n <= 16384:
            high_n = 32768
        elif base_n <= 32768:
            high_n = 65536
        else:
            high_n = base_n
        if high_n <= 65536:
            high = resample_sequence(best_a, high_n)
            high_sc = normalized_score(high)
            if np.isfinite(high_sc) and (high_sc <= best_sc * 1.004 or high_sc < 1.525):
                high_dead = min(hard_deadline - 80, t0 + 0.85 * total)
                if time.monotonic() < high_dead:
                    sc_high, seq_high = optimize_self(high, high_dead, rng, verbose=True, label="high")
                    if sc_high < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc_high
                        best_a = seq_high.copy()
                        print("High-n done: {:.10f}".format(best_sc))

    # Final gentle polishing and robust finish.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 15:
        polish_dead = min(hard_deadline - 15, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, 0.04 * total))
        if time.monotonic() < polish_dead:
            sc_pol, seq_pol = polish_self(best_a, polish_dead, rng, verbose=False, label="polish")
            if sc_pol < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_pol
                best_a = seq_pol.copy()

        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 15:
            sc_final, seq_final = optimize_self(best_a, hard_deadline - 10, rng,
                                                verbose=False, label="final")
            if sc_final < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_final
                best_a = seq_final.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_sc = normalized_score(best_a)

    # Final safety normalization: return a strictly non-negative finite list.
    out_arr = clean_nonnegative(best_a)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print("DONE: internal={:.10f} verified={:.10f} n={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t0))
    return out


def finalize_for_return(best_a):
    out_arr = clean_nonnegative(best_a)
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s
    return [float(v) for v in out_arr]
```
